`CODE_OF_CONDUCT.md`:

```md

# Contributor Covenant Code of Conduct

## Our Pledge

We as members, contributors, and leaders pledge to make participation in our community a harassment-free experience for everyone, regardless of age, body size, visible or invisible disability, ethnicity, sex characteristics, gender identity and expression, level of experience, education, socio-economic status, nationality, personal appearance, race, caste, color, religion, or sexual identity and orientation.

We pledge to act and interact in ways that contribute to an open, welcoming, diverse, inclusive, and healthy community.

## Our Standards

Examples of behavior that contributes to a positive environment for our community include:

* Demonstrating empathy and kindness toward other people
* Being respectful of differing opinions, viewpoints, and experiences
* Giving and gracefully accepting constructive feedback
* Accepting responsibility and apologizing to those affected by our mistakes, and learning from the experience
* Focusing on what is best not just for us as individuals, but for the overall community

Examples of unacceptable behavior include:

* The use of sexualized language or imagery, and sexual attention or advances of any kind
* Trolling, insulting or derogatory comments, and personal or political attacks
* Public or private harassment
* Publishing others' private information, such as a physical or email address, without their explicit permission
* Other conduct which could reasonably be considered inappropriate in a professional setting

## Enforcement Responsibilities

Community leaders are responsible for clarifying and enforcing our standards of acceptable behavior and will take appropriate and fair corrective action in response to any behavior that they deem inappropriate, threatening, offensive, or harmful.

Community leaders have the right and responsibility to remove, edit, or reject comments, commits, code, wiki edits, issues, and other contributions that are not aligned to this Code of Conduct, and will communicate reasons for moderation decisions when appropriate.

## Scope

This Code of Conduct applies within all community spaces, and also applies when an individual is officially representing the community in public spaces. Examples of representing our community include using an official e-mail address, posting via an official social media account, or acting as an appointed representative at an online or offline event.

## Enforcement

Instances of abusive, harassing, or otherwise unacceptable behavior may be reported to the community leaders responsible for enforcement at the maintainer via GitHub (@jammyfu) — open a private security advisory or contact the maintainer through their GitHub profile. All complaints will be reviewed and investigated promptly and fairly.

All community leaders are obligated to respect the privacy and security of the reporter of any incident.

## Enforcement Guidelines

Community leaders will follow these Community Impact Guidelines in determining the consequences for any action they deem in violation of this Code of Conduct:

### 1. Correction

**Community Impact**: Use of inappropriate language or other behavior deemed unprofessional or unwelcome in the community.

**Consequence**: A private, written warning from community leaders, providing clarity around the nature of the violation and an explanation of why the behavior was inappropriate. A public apology may be requested.

### 2. Warning

**Community Impact**: A violation through a single incident or series of actions.

**Consequence**: A warning with consequences for continued behavior. No interaction with the people involved, including unsolicited interaction with those enforcing the Code of Conduct, for a specified period of time. This includes avoiding interactions in community spaces as well as external channels like social media. Violating these terms may lead to a temporary or permanent ban.

### 3. Temporary Ban

**Community Impact**: A serious violation of community standards, including sustained inappropriate behavior.

**Consequence**: A temporary ban from any sort of interaction or public communication with the community for a specified period of time. No public or private interaction with the people involved, including unsolicited interaction with those enforcing the Code of Conduct, is allowed during this period. Violating these terms may lead to a permanent ban.

### 4. Permanent Ban

**Community Impact**: Demonstrating a pattern of violation of community standards, including sustained inappropriate behavior, harassment of an individual, or aggression toward or disparagement of classes of individuals.

**Consequence**: A permanent ban from any sort of public interaction within the community.

## Attribution

This Code of Conduct is adapted from the [Contributor Covenant][homepage], version 2.1, available at [https://www.contributor-covenant.org/version/2/1/code_of_conduct.html][v2.1].

Community Impact Guidelines were inspired by [Mozilla's code of conduct enforcement ladder][Mozilla CoC].

For answers to common questions about this code of conduct, see the FAQ at [https://www.contributor-covenant.org/faq][FAQ]. Translations are available at [https://www.contributor-covenant.org/translations][translations].

[homepage]: https://www.contributor-covenant.org
[v2.1]: https://www.contributor-covenant.org/version/2/1/code_of_conduct.html
[Mozilla CoC]: https://github.com/mozilla/diversity
[FAQ]: https://www.contributor-covenant.org/faq
[translations]: https://www.contributor-covenant.org/translations


```

`CONTRIBUTING.md`:

```md
# Contributing to REA Atlas

1. One entry per line: `- [owner/repo](url) — **Uses rea: Yes|No|Unverified**. One-sentence summary.`
2. **Uses rea: Yes** needs public evidence (README, repo docs, official rea.tools showcase). Link it in the PR.
3. Put entries under the right platform and type (Decompilation / Recomp, Remake / Port, Asset extraction & tools) or the non-game sections.
4. Not accepted: cracks, DRM bypass, piracy, projects that ship ROMs, game files or other copyrighted assets.
5. Links must return HTTP 200 (or a valid redirect).
6. Update `data.json` and all five READMEs (en, zh-CN, zh-TW, ja, ko) in the same PR; summaries stay in English.
7. Images: only redistributable images, with source and license credited.

By contributing you dedicate your contribution under CC0-1.0.

```

`GEO.md`:

```md
# GEO / repo metadata (jammyfu/rea-atlas)

**About:** Map of reverse-engineering projects (with rea tags): game decomp/recomp across NES→Switch-era consoles and PC, re-implementations, asset tools, RE tooling, app/protocol/firmware. Each tagged "Uses rea: Yes/No/Unverified". EN/中文/日本語/한국어.

**Topics:** reverse-engineering, decompilation, static-recompilation, game-preservation, reimplementation, rea, reverse-engineer-anything, ghidra, radare2, frida, firmware-analysis, mcp, retro-games, curated-list

```

`README.ja.md`:

```md
[English](README.md) · [简体中文](README.zh-CN.md) · [繁體中文](README.zh-TW.md) · [日本語](README.ja.md) · [한국어](README.ko.md)

# REA Atlas：リバースエンジニアリング・プロジェクトマップ（rea タグ付き）

オープンなリバースエンジニアリング・プロジェクトのマップです。ゲームの逆コンパイル、静的再コンパイル、再実装、アセット抽出ツール、汎用 RE ツール、アプリ／プロトコル／ファームウェア解析を収録し、各項目に [morluto/rea](https://github.com/morluto/rea)（Reverse Engineer Anything）の使用有無 **Uses rea: Yes / No / Unverified** を付けています。Yes は公開された根拠がある場合のみです。

> 非公式であり、morluto/rea とは無関係です。
```

```md
# REA Atlas: 리버스 엔지니어링 프로젝트 지도 (rea 태그 포함)

오픈 리버스 엔지니어링 프로젝트 지도입니다. 게임 디컴파일, 정적 리컴파일, 재구현, 에셋 추출 도구, 범용 RE 도구, 앱/프로토콜/펌웨어 분석을 수록하며, 각 항목에 [morluto/rea](https://github.com/morluto/rea)(Reverse Engineer Anything) 사용 여부 **Uses rea: Yes / No / Unverified**를 표시합니다. Yes는 공개 근거가 있을 때만 표시합니다.

> 비공식이며 morluto/rea와 관련이 없습니다.
```

```md
# REA Atlas: a map of reverse-engineering projects (with rea tags)

A map of open reverse-engineering projects — game decompilations, static recompilations, re-implementations, asset tools, general RE tooling, app/protocol/firmware work — each tagged **Uses rea: Yes / No / Unverified** for [morluto/rea](https://github.com/morluto/rea) (Reverse Engineer Anything). Yes only with public evidence (README, official showcase or repo docs).

> Unofficial; not affiliated with morluto/rea.
```

```md
# REA Atlas：逆向工程项目地图（含 rea 标记）

开源逆向工程项目地图：游戏反编译、静态重编译、重实现、资源提取工具、通用逆向工具，以及应用/协议/固件逆向。每条标注是否使用 [morluto/rea](https://github.com/morluto/rea)（Reverse Engineer Anything）：**Uses rea: Yes / No / Unverified**，只有公开证据（README、官方 showcase 或仓库文档）才标 Yes。

> 非官方，与 morluto/rea 无关联。
```

```md
# REA Atlas：逆向工程專案地圖（含 rea 標記）

開源逆向工程專案地圖：遊戲反編譯、靜態重編譯、重新實作、資源擷取工具、通用逆向工具，以及應用程式/協定/韌體逆向。每條標註是否使用 [morluto/rea](https://github.com/morluto/rea)（Reverse Engineer Anything）：**Uses rea: Yes / No / Unverified**，只有在具備公開證據（README、官方 showcase 或儲存庫文件）才標 Yes。

> 非官方，與 morluto/rea 無關聯。
```

`SECURITY.md`:

```md
# Security Policy

## Reporting a vulnerability

Please **do not** open a public issue for security problems.
Use GitHub's private reporting: **Security → Report a vulnerability** on
https://github.com/jammyfu/rea-atlas/security/advisories/new

We aim to acknowledge reports within 7 days. Only the latest `main` is supported.

```

`llms.txt`:

```txt
# REA Atlas — a map of reverse-engineering projects (with rea tags)

> Map of open reverse-engineering projects: game decompilations and static recompilations across NES, SNES, Mega Drive, Saturn, GB, GBA, N64, GameCube, Wii, DS, 3DS, PS1, PS2, Xbox and PC; re-implementations; asset tools; general RE tooling (Ghidra, radare2, Rizin, x64dbg, Frida, angr, ImHex, IDA MCP); app, protocol and firmware work. Each entry is labelled "Uses rea: Yes / No / Unverified" for morluto/rea (https://github.com/morluto/rea). No ROMs, cracks or copyrighted assets.

## Docs
- [README (English)](README.md)
- [简体中文](README.zh-CN.md) · [繁體中文](README.zh-TW.md) · [日本語](README.ja.md) · [한국어](README.ko.md)
- [Machine-readable entries](data.json), [images](images.json): [section, subsection, name, url, uses_rea, note]
- [Contributing](CONTRIBUTING.md)

## Sections
- Official REA showcases; Games by platform (NES, SNES, Mega Drive, Saturn, GB, GBA, N64, GameCube, Wii, DS, 3DS, PS1, PS2, Xbox, PC) split into Decompilation/Recomp, Remake/Port, Asset tools; Apps/Electron; Protocols; Firmware; Security; Tooling.

```

```py
X={
'en':dict(toc="Contents",img="Image",proj="Project",desc="What you get",rea="Uses rea",src="Image source",
  K=dict(readme="Project README",site="Project site",issue="Maintainer post",wiki="Wikipedia (© owner)",shot="Our screenshot",video="Video thumbnail",card="Repo card",none="—"),
  Y=dict(Yes="✅ Yes",No="No",Unverified="❔ Unverified")),

  Y=dict(Yes="✅ はい",No="いいえ",Unverified="❔ 未確認")),
'ko':dict(toc="목차",img="이미지",proj="프로젝트",desc="소개",rea="rea 사용",src="이미지 출처",
  K=dict(readme="프로젝트 README",site="프로젝트 사이트",issue="메인테이너 게시물",wiki="위키백과(© 권리자)",shot="자체 스크린샷",video="동영상 썸네일",card="저장소 카드",none="—"),
  Y=dict(Yes="✅ 예",No="아니요",Unverified="❔ 미확인")),
}
def slug(h,seen):
  s=re.sub(r'[^\w\- ]','',h.strip().lower()).replace(' ','-')
  n=seen.get(s,0);seen[s]=n+1
  return s if n==0 else f'{s}-{n}'
def table(d,x,es,k):
  r=['<table>',f'<tr><th width="170">{x["img"]}</th><th>{x["proj"]}</th><th>{x["desc"]}</th><th>{x["rea"]}</th><th>{x["src"]}</th></tr>']
  for e in es:
    im=IM.get(e[2]);kind=im[0] if im else 'none'
    pic=f'<a href="{e[3]}"><img src="{html.escape(im[1])}" width="160" alt="{html.escape(e[2])}"></a>' if im else '—'
    src=x['K'][kind]
    if kind in('wiki','video','issue') and im and kind!='issue': src=f'<a href="{html.escape(im[1])}">{src}</a>'
    nm=e[2].split('/')[-1] if '/' in e[2] and ' ' not in e[2] else e[2]
    own=f'<br><sub>{e[2].split("/")[0]}</sub>' if '/' in e[2] and ' ' not in e[2] else ''
    r.append(f'<tr><td>{pic}</td><td><a href="{e[3]}"><b>{html.escape(nm)}</b></a>{own}</td><td>{html.escape(DS[e[2]][k])}</td><td>{x["Y"][e[4]]}</td><td><sub>{src}</sub></td></tr>')
  r.append('</table>');return r
for k,d in L.items():
  x=X[k];seen={};body=[];toc=[]
  def H(level,t):
    toc.append((level,t,slug(t,seen)));body.extend(['#'*level+' '+t,''])
  H(2,d['sec']['games'])
  for p in plats:
    es=[e for e in E if e[0]==p]
    if not es:continue
    H(3,d['sec'].get(p,d['sec'].get('_'+p)))
    for s in ['decomp','remake','tools']:
      ss=[e for e in es if e[1]==s]
      if ss:
        t=d['sub'][s];slug(t,seen);body+=[f'#### {t}','']+table(d,x,ss,k)+['']
  for s in ['app','protocol','firmware','security','tooling','official']:
    es=[e for e in E if e[0]==s]
    if es:H(2,d['sec'][s]);body+=table(d,x,es,k)+['']
  head=[bar,"",f"# {d['t']}","",d['tag'],"","> "+re.sub(r'(?<=[.。])[^.。]*[.。]$','',d['disc']).strip(),""]
  tl=[f"## {x['toc']}",""]+[("  "*(l-2))+f"- [{t}](#{s})" for l,t,s in toc]+[""]
  slug(x['toc'],seen)
  o=head+tl+body+["---","",d['thx'],"",d['c'],""]
  open(d['f'],'w').write("\n".join(o))
print('ok',len(E))

```
